Our work advances scalable visual analysis for HPC monitoring and has broader implications for cloud, edge computing, and distributed infrastructures where interpretability and behavior analysis are critical to operational efficiency.

</details>


### [90] [Predictive Bayesian Arbitration: A Scalable Noisy-OR Model with Service Criticality Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11989)
*Anil Jangam,Ganesh Karthick Rajendran,Roy Kantharajah*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于微服务共享架构的预测性仲裁框架，利用贝叶斯Noisy-OR模型自适应学习故障级联依赖，实现了故障的主动预测与切换。实验表明，该方法将故障检测平均时间（MTTFD）减少了60%，切换效率提升高达77.8%，同时保持了线性计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统地理高可用（Geo-HA）集群系统的仲裁机制依赖确定性的节点级心跳，存在资源消耗大、反应被动、需要专用仲裁节点以及故障发生后才进行切换导致不可避免停机时间等问题。

Method: 提出一种新颖的预测性仲裁框架，采用共享的微服务架构整合多个Geo-HA域的仲裁逻辑；核心是利用贝叶斯Noisy-OR模型构建自适应在线学习机制，自主发现和学习时序级联依赖关系；通过专家先验知识解决“冷启动”问题，并在运行时动态优化。

Result: 与传统反应式标准相比，故障检测平均时间（MTTFD）减少60%，总体切换效率提升高达77.8%，计算复杂度为线性O(n)；系统能够在硬故障发生前主动启动切换。

Conclusion: 该方法为现代微服务架构提供了一种可扩展、上下文感知的仲裁替代方案，弥合了性能与持久性之间的差距，实现了故障前的主动预测性切换。

Abstract: Geographically High-Available (Geo-HA) cluster systems are essential for service continuity in distributed cloud-native environments. However, traditional arbitration mechanisms, which are often predicated on deterministic node-level heartbeats, are resource-intensive and inherently reactive. This necessitates a dedicated arbiter per deployment and leads to reactive switchovers that incur unavoidable downtime, occurring only after a failure has already compromised the system. This paper presents a novel predictive arbitration framework that utilizes a shared, microservice-based architecture to consolidate arbitration logic across multiple Geo-HA domains, significantly reducing the aggregate infrastructure footprint. Central to our approach is an adaptive online learning mechanism grounded in a Bayesian Noisy-OR model that autonomously discovers and learns temporal cascade dependencies from emergent failure patterns. To overcome the "cold start" challenge, the system utilizes expert-informed priors that are dynamically refined at runtime without manual configuration. Experimental results demonstrate that this framework achieves a 60\% reduction in Mean Time to Failure Detection (MTTFD) and improves total switchover efficiency by up to 77.8\% compared to traditional reactive standards. By enabling a significant predictive lead time, the system allows switchovers to initiate proactively before hard failures occur, while maintaining a linear $O(n)$ computational complexity. This approach provides a scalable, context-aware alternative that bridges the performance-durability gap in modern microservice architectures.

</details>


### [91] [Accelerating Microswimmer Simulations via a Heterogeneous Pipelined Parallel-in-Time Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12083)
*Ruixiang Huang,Weifan Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对粘性流体中丝状微游泳体长时间模拟的异构CPU-GPU计算框架，通过两级并行策略（GPU加速空间计算、MPI-GPU流水线Parareal架构利用时间并发）实现了比纯CPU方法高一个数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模微游泳体在粘性流体中的动力学模拟面临高空间和时间复杂度的双重挑战，而传统高性能计算（HPC）方法通常孤立地处理这两个维度，缺乏协同加速机制，存在显著的计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出了两级并行化策略：（1）利用高强度GPU内核求解正则化Stokeslet方法（MRS）中的二次空间相互作用；（2）采用分布式MPI-GPU流水线Parareal架构开发时间并发性，通过将异步流水线映射到多个GPU设备，有效重叠粗细传播器，克服了传统Parareal方法的串行瓶颈。此外，还采用了GPU优化的数值例程来计算模拟中出现的矩阵平方根。

Result: 数值实验表明，该框架相比纯CPU方法实现了数量级的加速，理论分析也验证了流水线Parareal方法的效率提升。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了空间与时间维度的协同加速，为模拟大规模生物和物理系统中复杂涌现行为提供了一条可扩展的计算途径。

Abstract: Simulating large-scale microswimmer dynamics in viscous fluid poses significant challenges due to the coupled high spatial and temporal complexity. Conventional high-performance computing (HPC) methods often address these two dimensions in isolation, leaving a critical gap for synergistic acceleration. This paper introduces a heterogeneous CPU--GPU computing framework specifically optimized for the long-time simulation of filamentous microswimmers in viscous fluid. We propose a two-level parallelization strategy: (1) high-intensity GPU kernels to resolve the quadratic spatial interactions given by the Method of Regularized Stokeslets (MRS), and (2) a distributed MPI-GPU pipelined Parareal architecture to exploit temporal concurrency. By mapping the asynchronous pipeline onto multiple GPU devices, our framework effectively overlaps coarse and fine propagators, overcoming the serial bottlenecks of traditional Parareal method. Furthermore, we employ a GPU-optimized numerical routine for computing the matrix square root arising in the numerical scheme of the filamentous microswimmer simulations. Theoretical analysis of the efficiency improvement of the pipelined Parareal is presented. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves order-of-magnitude speedups over CPU-only methods, providing a scalable pathway for simulating complex emergent behaviors in large-scale biology and physics systems.

</details>


### [92] [Evaluating Cross-Architecture Performance Modeling of Distributed ML Workloads Using StableHLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12090)
*Jonas Svedas,Nathan Laubeuf,Ryan Harvey,Arjun Singh,Changhai Man,Abubakr Nada,Tushar Krishna,James Myers,Debjyoti Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于MLIR StableHLO方言的统一仿真方法，实现了跨GPU和TPU架构的分布式机器学习性能预测，无需依赖大规模物理系统即可完成不同保真度模型的系统性比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GPU和TPU模拟器通常局限于特定架构，而分布式训练模拟器依赖于特定工作负载的分析模型或昂贵的执行后追踪，限制了可移植性和跨平台比较能力。跨多个加速器架构预测大规模分布式机器学习工作负载的性能仍然是机器学习系统设计中的核心挑战。

Method: 建立基于StableHLO的仿真方法论，将单一工作负载表示映射到多种性能模型上，涵盖分析型、基于性能剖析和模拟器驱动的预测器。该方法无需访问大规模物理系统即可将工作负载在GPU和TPU上进行评估，实现了跨建模保真度的系统性比较。

Result: 在分布式GEMM内核、ResNet和大语言模型训练工作负载上的实证评估表明，StableHLO在不同架构和保真度之间保持了相对性能趋势，同时揭示了精度权衡和模拟器局限性。预测误差在早期设计探索的实际范围内，研究还揭示了现有GPU模拟器中依赖于保真度的局限性。

Conclusion: StableHLO为跨加速器架构和模拟器的统一分布式机器学习性能建模提供了可行的基础，支持可复用的评估工作流程和机器学习系统设计过程中的交叉验证。

Abstract: Predicting the performance of large-scale distributed machine learning (ML) workloads across multiple accelerator architectures remains a central challenge in ML system design. Existing GPU and TPU focused simulators are typically architecture-specific, while distributed training simulators rely on workload-specific analytical models or costly post-execution traces, limiting portability and cross-platform comparison. This work evaluates whether MLIR's StableHLO dialect can serve as a unified workload representation for cross-architecture and cross-fidelity performance modeling of distributed ML workloads. The study establishes a StableHLO-based simulation methodology that maps a single workload representation onto multiple performance models, spanning analytical, profiling-based, and simulator-driven predictors. Using this methodology, workloads are evaluated across GPUs and TPUs without requiring access to scaled-out physical systems, enabling systematic comparison across modeling fidelities. An empirical evaluation covering distributed GEMM kernels, ResNet, and large language model training workloads demonstrates that StableHLO preserves relative performance trends across architectures and fidelities, while exposing accuracy trade-offs and simulator limitations. Across evaluated scenarios, prediction errors remain within practical bounds for early-stage design exploration, and the methodology reveals fidelity-dependent limitations in existing GPU simulators. These results indicate that StableHLO provides a viable foundation for unified, distributed ML performance modeling across accelerator architectures and simulators, supporting reusable evaluation workflows and cross-validation throughout the ML system design process.

</details>


### [93] [PipeLive: Efficient Live In-place Pipeline Parallelism Reconfiguration for Dynamic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12171)
*Xu Bai,Muhammed Tawfiqul Islam,Chen Wang,Adel N. Toosi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PipeLive系统实现了大型语言模型流水线并行（PP）的实时原位重配置。通过重新设计KV缓存布局和扩展PageAttention，结合增量KV补丁机制，将重配置开销从秒级降至10毫秒以下，并将TTFT降低多达54.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流水线并行（PP）系统依赖静态配置，无法适应无服务器平台和异构GPU等动态环境。传统的停止并重新部署服务的重配置方式会导致不可接受的停机时间。然而，实现实时原位PP重配置面临两大核心挑战：1）GPU已被模型权重和KV缓存占满，缺乏空间进行新的层放置，需要进行KV缓存调整，但这与vLLM等系统为吞吐量预分配的策略相冲突；2）在执行过程中维护KV一致性极其困难，停止-复制会引入长暂停，而后台同步则面临状态演变导致不一致的风险。

Method: PipeLive提出了三项关键技术：1）重新设计的KV缓存布局，并协同扩展了PageAttention机制，形成统一的实时KV调整机制，解决了空间受限下的缓存重分配问题；2）借鉴虚拟机实时迁移的思想，采用增量KV补丁机制，在源配置和目标配置之间同步KV状态，并识别安全的切换点，从而在保持一致性的同时最小化停顿；3）整体实现了无需中断推理的实时原位重配置流程。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）与禁用KV调整的方案相比，PipeLive在无KV缓存溢出的情况下，将首字延迟（TTFT）降低了2.5倍；2）与无KV补丁的变体相比，将重配置开销从秒级降低至10毫秒以下；3）TTFT和每输出词元时间（TPOT）分别改善了最高54.7%和14.7%。

Conclusion: PipeLive成功解决了动态环境下LLM推理服务的流水线并行实时重配置难题，证明了在GPU资源饱和和严格一致性要求下，实现低延迟、低开销的实时原位重配置是可行的。该系统使LLM推理能够高效适应异构GPU环境和无服务器平台等动态场景，显著提升了服务的响应速度和资源利用效率。

Abstract: Pipeline parallelism (PP) is widely used to partition layers of large language models (LLMs) across GPUs, enabling scalable inference for large models. However, existing systems rely on static PP configurations that fail to adapt to dynamic settings, such as serverless platforms and heterogeneous GPU environments. Reconfiguring PP by stopping and redeploying service incurs prohibitive downtime, so reconfiguration must instead proceed live and in place, without interrupting inference. However, live in-place PP reconfiguration is fundamentally challenging. GPUs are already saturated with model weights and KV cache, leaving little room for new layer placements and necessitating KV cache resizing, at odds with systems like vLLM that preallocate for throughput. Moreover, maintaining KV consistency during execution is difficult: stop-and-copy introduces large pauses, while background synchronization risks inconsistency as states evolve. We present PipeLive, which enables live in-place PP reconfiguration with minimal disruption. PipeLive introduces a redesigned KV cache layout together with a co-designed extension to PageAttention, forming a unified mechanism for live KV resizing. It further adopts an incremental KV patching mechanism, inspired by live virtual machine migration, to synchronize KV states between source and target configurations and identify a safe switch point. PipeLive achieves a 2.5X reduction in time-to-first-token (TTFT) without KV cache overflow compared to disabling KV resizing. Furthermore, compared to a variant without KV patching, it reduces reconfiguration overhead from seconds to under 10ms, and improves TTFT and time-per-output-token (TPOT) by up to 54.7% and 14.7%, respectively.

</details>


### [94] [BlazingAML: High-Throughput Anti-Money Laundering (AML) via Multi-Stage Graph Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12241)
*Haojie Ye,Arjun Laxman,Yichao Yuan,Krisztian Flautner,Nishil Talati*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: BlazingAML系统通过多阶段框架表达模糊洗钱模式，配合领域特定编译器实现CPU上210倍、GPU上333倍的加速，在保持F1分数的同时显著提升了反洗钱检测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反洗钱检测面临过多误报问题，且难以适应利用现代金融网络的复杂多阶段洗钱方案。图分析和AI方法受限于洗钱模式的结构和时间变化性（模糊性），传统数据挖掘技术需要详细枚举模式变体，导致分析员任务复杂、运行时开销大且难以训练准确的AI模型。

Method: 提出BlazingAML系统，包含两个核心创新：(1) 新颖的多阶段框架，用于表达模糊洗钱模式，将复杂洗钱方案分解为通过图操作连接的逻辑阶段；(2) 领域特定编译器，将高层模式描述转换为CPU和GPU后端的高性能代码，应用复杂优化并消除手动并行编程需求。多阶段抽象使用统一原语捕获结构和时间模糊性。

Result: 在IBM AML数据集上的评估显示，BlazingAML在与最先进方法达到相同F1分数的同时，在CPU和GPU上分别实现了210倍和333倍的加速，并展现出优越的可扩展性。

Conclusion: BlazingAML成功解决了反洗钱检测中的模糊模式表达和性能瓶颈问题，通过创新的抽象框架和编译器技术，在保证检测准确性的前提下大幅提升了处理效率，为金融分析师提供了实用的反洗钱解决方案。

Abstract: Money laundering detection faces challenges due to excessive false positives and inadequate adaptation to sophisticated multi-stage schemes that exploit modern financial networks. Graph analytics and AI are promising tools, but they struggle with the fuzziness of laundering patterns, which exhibit structural and temporal variations. Conventional data mining techniques require the detailed enumeration of pattern variants, which not only complicates the analyst's task to specify them, but also leads to large run-time overheads and difficulty training accurate AI models. The paper presents BlazingAML, a scalable AML system design that introduces: 1. A novel multi-stage framework for expressing fuzzy money laundering patterns 2. A domain-specific compiler that transforms high-level pattern descriptions into high-performance code for CPU and GPU back-ends The multi-stage abstraction decomposes complex laundering schemes into logical stages connected by graph operations, enabling diverse patterns to be expressed using unified primitives while capturing structural and temporal fuzziness. The compiler applies sophisticated optimizations, eliminating manual parallel programming requirements for financial analysts. Evaluation on IBM AML datasets shows BlazingAML achieves the same F1 score as state-of-the-art approaches while delivering 210x and 333x higher speedup on CPU and GPU respectively, with superior scalability.

</details>


### [95] [Local-Splitter: A Measurement Study of Seven Tactics for Reducing Cloud LLM Token Usage on Coding-Agent Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12301)
*Justice Owusu Agyemang,Jerry John Kponyo,Elliot Amponsah,Godfred Manu Addo Boakye,Kwame Opuni-Boachie Obour Agyekum*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究评估了七种降低云大模型Token使用量的策略，发现在编辑密集型和解释密集型任务中，本地路由结合提示压缩可节省45-79%的云Token，且最优策略组合依赖于具体工作负载类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云大模型的Token使用成本高昂，若在云端模型前引入小型本地模型作为分流层，可能显著降低调用成本。本文旨在系统评估多种Token节省策略的实际效果，为开发者提供优化指导。

Method: 在开源代理层中实现了七种节省Token的策略（本地路由、提示压缩、语义缓存、本地起草-云端审查、最小差异编辑、结构化意图提取、批处理与供应商提示缓存），支持MCP与OpenAI兼容接口；针对四类编程代理工作负载（编辑密集、解释密集、通用对话、RAG密集）分别测试了各策略单独、成对及贪心叠加组合的效果，测量Token节省量、成本、延迟与路由准确率。

Result: 本地路由（T1）与提示压缩（T2）组合在编辑密集和解释密集型工作负载上实现45-79%的云Token节省；在RAG密集型工作负载中，包含本地起草-云端审查（T4）在内的全策略集实现51%节省。最优策略子集依赖于具体工作负载类型。

Conclusion: 不同编程代理任务类型对应不同的最优Token节省策略组合，实践中应根据工作负载特征选择合适策略，以实现最佳成本效益。

Abstract: We present a systematic measurement study of seven tactics for reducing cloud LLM token usage when a small local model can act as a triage layer in front of a frontier cloud model. The tactics are: (1) local routing, (2) prompt compression, (3) semantic caching, (4) local drafting with cloud review, (5) minimal-diff edits, (6) structured intent extraction, and (7) batching with vendor prompt caching. We implement all seven in an open-source shim that speaks both MCP and the OpenAI-compatible HTTP surface, supporting any local model via Ollama and any cloud model via an OpenAI-compatible endpoint. We evaluate each tactic individually, in pairs, and in a greedy-additive subset across four coding-agent workload classes (edit-heavy, explanation-heavy, general chat, RAG-heavy). We measure tokens saved, dollar cost, latency, and routing accuracy. Our headline finding is that T1 (local routing) combined with T2 (prompt compression) achieves 45-79% cloud token savings on edit-heavy and explanation-heavy workloads, while on RAG-heavy workloads the full tactic set including T4 (draft-review) achieves 51% savings. We observe that the optimal tactic subset is workload-dependent, which we believe is the most actionable finding for practitioners deploying coding agents today.

</details>


### [96] [Three Birds, One Stone: Solving the Communication-Memory-Privacy Trilemma in LLM Fine-tuning Over Wireless Networks with Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12401)
*Zhijie Cai,Yuhao Zheng,Haolong Chen,Dongzhu Liu,Bin Wang,Guangxu Zhu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出pAirZero框架，结合零阶优化与空中计算技术，在联邦学习中实现大语言模型的高效安全微调，显著降低通信负载、内存开销并保护隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在边缘设备上微调大语言模型面临三大瓶颈：高维梯度交换导致的通信和内存开销过大，以及本地梯度可能泄露用户训练数据的隐私风险。传统方法难以同时解决通信、内存和隐私的三角困境。

Method: 提出pAirZero框架，将零阶（Zeroth-Order）优化与空中计算（Over-the-Air computation）相结合。该框架使资源受限设备仅需比特级通信负载即可提交本地梯度，并以推理级内存成本参与大语言模型联邦微调。同时建立严格的优化模型，自适应确定最优发射功率和噪声水平，确保在不同信道条件下的一致性隐私保护。

Result: 实验表明，pAirZero在OPT-125M模型上仅需25%的峰值内存成本，通信负载比传统方法低数个数量级，成功实现了无线网络上安全高效的大语言模型微调。该方法消除了大语言模型微调的高内存需求，同时缓解了传统空中计算方法的严格同步要求。

Conclusion: pAirZero框架有效解决了联邦学习微调大语言模型中通信、内存和隐私的三难问题，为资源受限的边缘设备参与大语言模型协作训练提供了可行方案，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) offers a promising pathway for collaboratively fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) at the edge; however, this paradigm faces a critical bottleneck: the prohibitive communication and memory overheads incurred by exchanging high-dimensional gradients. Furthermore, recent studies reveal that user training data can still be recovered from these local gradients, undermining the core privacy promise of FL. In this paper, we address this trilemma of communication, memory, and privacy by proposing pAirZero, a novel framework that synergizes Zeroth-Order (ZO) optimization with Over-the-Air (OTA) computation. Uniquely, pAirZero enables resource-constrained devices to submit their local gradient with only bit-level communication loads while participating in federated fine-tuning of LLMs with inference-level memory costs. This approach not only eliminates the high memory requirements needed for LLM fine-tuning but also alleviates the strict synchronization requirements that plague conventional OTA methods. We further formulate a rigorous optimization model to adaptively determine the optimal transmit power and noise levels, ensuring consistent privacy protection regardless of channel conditions. Numerical experiments demonstrate the superiority of pAirZero in enabling secure, efficient LLM fine-tuning over wireless networks, with only 25% peak memory cost on OPT-125M and communication load orders of magnitude lower than conventional methods.

</details>


### [97] [Beyond Pre-Training: The Full Lifecycle of Foundation Models on HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12599)
*Dino Conciatore,Elia Oggian,Federico Da Forno,Stefano Schuppli,Jerome Tissieres,Joost VandeVondele,Maxime Martinasso*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在瑞士国家超级计算中心(CSCS)部署的混合云原生平台架构，该平台结合了无盘GPU节点与虚拟化基础设施，由Kubernetes编排管理，旨在解决在国家超算设施中完整运行AI生命周期（预训练、微调、推理）的架构与运营挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型的大规模预训练推动了HPC设施成为"主权AI"计划的核心，但AI生命周期的后续阶段（微调和推理）具有与传统HPC批处理环境不同的运营范式和资源利用模式，需要大量高端计算资源。现有超算设施难以有效支持完整的AI生命周期，存在架构和运营层面的冲突。

Method: 开发并部署混合云原生平台，将无盘GPU加速的HPE Cray EX计算节点与虚拟化通用基础设施相结合，使用Kubernetes进行编排调度。该服务架构在HPC批处理与服务导向工作流之间建立桥梁，支持微调流水线和高可用推理服务。

Result: 初步研究了微调流水线和高可用推理服务的实现，分析了相关权衡，同时提升了用户生产力。为超算设施集成"AI工厂"服务和工作流提供了实践性的蓝图方案。

Conclusion: 该混合云原生架构能够有效弥合HPC批处理与服务导向工作流之间的差距，使超级计算机能够支持端到端的科学和工业AI应用场景，为国家级超算中心运营完整AI生命周期提供了可行的技术路径和参考方案。

Abstract: Large-scale pre-training of Foundational Models (FM) constitutes a computationally intensive first phase for enabling AI across diverse scientific and societal applications. This first phase has positioned High-Performance Computing (HPC) facilities as indispensable backbones of "Sovereign AI" initiatives. While the massive throughput requirements of FM pre-training align with the traditional capability-oriented mission of HPC, subsequent phases of the AI lifecycle, typically referred to as fine-tuning and inference, introduce operational paradigms that can conflict with established batch-processing environments. Moreover, these phases are not computationally trivial: they often require substantial high-end compute resources while exhibiting hardware utilization patterns that differ significantly from those of pre-training.
  This paper addresses the architectural and strategic challenges of operationalizing a complete AI lifecycle within a national supercomputing facility. We present a hybrid cloud-native platform being developed and deployed at the Swiss National Supercomputing Centre (CSCS) that combines diskless GPU-enabled HPE Cray EX compute nodes with virtualized commodity infrastructure. Orchestrated by Kubernetes, this novel service architecture bridges the gap between HPC batch processing and service-oriented workflows. We report our initial investigations into fine-tuning pipelines and highly available inference services, analyzing the associated trade-offs while improving user productivity. Our findings offer a blueprint for enabling supercomputers to integrate "AI Factories" services and workflows, supporting AI innovations into end-to-end scientific and industrial use cases.

</details>


### [98] [Intelligent resource prediction for SAP HANA continuous integration build workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12673)
*Torsten Mandel,Jonathan Bader,Hanyoung Yoo,Stephan Kraft*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对企业CI流水线中静态资源配置导致的内存过度分配问题，提出了一种基于LightGBM-XGBoost分位数回归集成的内存预测方法，在生产环境中实现了平均每次构建节省约36GB内存，同时将内存分配不足率降至0.3%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型企业在异构计算集群上运行大规模CI流水线时，通常采用保守的静态资源配置以确保构建可靠性。通过对生产环境中超过30万次历史构建执行的分析，发现平均超过60%的已分配系统内存处于闲置状态，导致资源严重浪费、集群利用率降低和运营成本增加。

Method: 对比了多种机器学习方法预测构建任务内存使用量，包括基于分类的方法和基于回归的分位数预测方法。最终方案采用LightGBM-XGBoost分位数回归集成模型，优化目标是最小化内存分配不足的同时减少过度供给，并通过基于微服务的编排层集成到生产CI流水线中。

Result: 在生产环境部署中，平均每次构建节省约36GB内存，内存分配不足率降至0.3%以下，且未对构建执行时间产生负面影响。

Conclusion: 智能资源预测方法能有效解决CI流水线中的内存过度分配问题，显著提升集群资源利用率并降低运营成本，同时保持构建的高可靠性和执行效率。

Abstract: Large enterprises often operate extensive Continuous Integration (CI) pipelines on large, heterogeneous compute clusters, where conservative, statically defined resource requirements are used to ensure build reliability. This practice leads to substantial system memory over-allocation, reduced cluster utilization, and increased operational costs. In this paper, we motivate the need for intelligent resource prediction by analyzing over 300,000 historical build executions from a production CI environment with more than one thousand compute nodes. Our analysis shows that, on average, more than 60% of allocated system memory remains unused. We then compare multiple machine learning approaches for predicting build task memory usage, including classification-based methods and regression-based quantile prediction. Our final solution employs a LightGBM-XGBoost quantile regression ensemble optimized to minimize under-allocation while reducing over-provisioning. We integrate this solution into the production CI pipeline via a microservice-based orchestration layer, achieving average memory savings of approximately 36GB per build and reducing under-allocation rates to below 0.3% without negatively impacting build execution times.

</details>


### [99] [An Engineering Journey Training Large Language Models at Scale on Alps: The Apertus Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12973)
*Jonathan Coles,Stefano Schuppli,Lukas Drescher,Fawzi Roberto Mohamed,Elia Palme,Henrique Mendonça,Miguel Gila,Mark Klein,Maxime Martinasso,Joost VandeVondele,Torsten Hoefler,Thomas Schulthess,Josh Romero,Igor Gorodetsky,Ryan Hankins,Isa Wazirzada,Martin Jaggi,Antoine Bosselut,Imanol Schlag,Antoni-Joan Solergibert i Llaquet,Alejandro Hernández Cano,Theofilos Ioannis Manitaras,Nicholas John Browning*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在Alps超级计算机上成功训练首个学术界规模的70B参数开源多语言基础模型Apertus的工程实践，解决了HPC基础设施适配AI训练的挑战，为公共机构建立持续运营的机器学习平台奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型对科学和社会具有变革性影响，各国政府追求主权AI能力以确保数据合规和文化代表性。然而，训练这些模型所需的高昂资金成本和工程复杂性使其主要局限于私营部门，公共机构在这一领域存在显著空白。

Method: 在由NVIDIA GH200 Grace Hopper Superchips驱动的欧洲最大开放科学系统之一——Alps超级计算机上，部署大规模预训练活动，训练完全开源的多语言基础模型Apertus（70B参数规模）。解决了存储瓶颈、大规模互联稳定性等HPC基础设施问题，将超级计算机转化为弹性软件定义的机器学习平台。

Result: 成功实现了学术界首个70B参数规模的开源多语言基础模型Apertus的训练，这是学术界在该规模上的首次成就。克服了HPC基础设施适配AI训练的技术挑战，建立了可持续运营的机器学习平台能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为公共机构建立持续、迭代的运营能力奠定了基础，特别是针对基础模型微调的能力，这种能力远超单次模型训练运行的范畴，支持长期演进发展。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have surged as a transformative technology for science and society, prompting governments worldwide to pursue sovereign AI capabilities that ensure data compliance and cultural representation. However, the associated capital costs and engineering complexity required to train these models have largely restricted such capabilities to the private sector, leaving a significant gap for public institutions.
  This paper details the engineering journey behind training \textit{Apertus}, a fully open multilingual foundation model, on the \textit{Alps} supercomputer. Representing a first-of-its-kind achievement for academia at the 70B parameter scale, we successfully deployed a massive pre-training campaign on one of Europe's largest systems for open science, powered by NVIDIA GH200 Grace Hopper Superchips. We detail the challenges encountered in readying HPC infrastructure for training AI models, from overcoming storage bottlenecks to stabilizing large-scale interconnects, and the lessons learned in transforming a supercomputer into a resilient software-defined Machine Learning Platform.
  Finally, we discuss the post-training requirements and evolution of our Machine Learning platform, outlining how this initial release lays the groundwork for a sustained, iterative operational capability, in particular for fine tuning foundation models, that extends well beyond a single model training run.

</details>


### [100] [DySkew: Dynamic Data Redistribution for Skew-Resilient Snowpark UDF Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13034)
*Chenwei Xie,Urjeet Shrestha,Corbin McElhanney,Lukas Lorimer,Gopal V,Zihao Ye,Yi Pan,Nic Crouch,Elliott Brossard,Florian Funke,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了DySkew，一种针对Snowpark UDF的数据倾斜感知执行策略，通过自适应数据分布机制、急切重分发策略和行大小模型，有效解决了数据倾斜导致的性能下降问题，显著提升了大规模UDF工作负载的执行效率和资源利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Snowpark的UDF执行模型虽然具有高吞吐量，但极易受到数据倾斜的影响，不均匀的数据分区会导致straggler任务和不可预测的延迟。任意用户代码的非均匀计算成本进一步加剧了这一经典挑战，需要一种能够进行细粒度逐行缓解、动态运行时适应和低开销成本感知重分发的解决方案。

Method: 本文提出了DySkew策略，基于Snowflake新的通用倾斜处理解决方案——一种利用每链路状态机的自适应数据分布机制。针对Snowpark引入了关键优化，包括：(1)急切重分发策略；(2)行大小模型(Row Size Model)，用于动态管理极大行的开销；(3)动态方法取代了先前静态轮询方法的局限性。

Result: 通过性能评估和真实案例研究，证明了该框架的有效性，在大规模Snowpark UDF工作负载中，执行时间和资源利用率均获得了显著改善。

Conclusion: DySkew成功解决了Snowpark UDF执行中的数据倾斜问题，实现了细粒度、动态自适应且低开销的数据倾斜缓解，为大规模数据分析工作负载提供了更高效、更可靠的执行保障。

Abstract: Snowflake revolutionized data warehousing with an elastic architecture that decouples compute and storage, enabling scalable solutions for diverse data analytics needs. Building on this foundation, Snowflake has advanced its AI Data Cloud vision by introducing Snowpark, a managed turnkey solution that supports data engineering and AI/ML workloads using Python and other programming languages. While Snowpark's User-Defined Function (UDF) execution model offers high throughput, it is highly vulnerable to performance degradation from data skew, where uneven data partitioning causes straggler tasks and unpredictable latency. The non-uniform computational cost of arbitrary user code further exacerbates this classic challenge. This paper presents DySkew, a novel, data-skew-aware execution strategy for Snowpark UDFs. Built upon Snowflake's new generalized skew handling solution, an adaptive data distribution mechanism utilizing per-link state machines. DySkew addresses the unique challenges of user-defined logic with goals of fine-grained per-row mitigation, dynamic runtime adaptation, and low-overhead, cost-aware redistribution. Specifically, for Snowpark, we introduce crucial optimizations, including an eager redistribution strategy and a Row Size Model to dynamically manage overhead for extremely large rows. This dynamic approach replaces the limitations of the previous static round-robin method. We detail the architecture of this framework and showcase its effectiveness through performance evaluations and real-world case studies, demonstrating significant improvements in the execution time and resource utilization for large-scale Snowpark UDF workloads.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Uncertainty Quantification in CNN Through the Bootstrap of Convex Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11833)
*Hongfei Du,Emre Barut,Fang Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Bootstrap的新型框架，用于估计卷积神经网络（CNN）的预测不确定性。该方法利用凸化神经网络建立了理论一致性保证，并通过迁移学习扩展至任意神经网络结构，在大幅降低计算成本的同时，显著提升了不确定性估计的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管卷积神经网络（CNN）应用广泛，但其不确定性量化（UQ）问题长期被忽视。现有的深度学习UQ方法缺乏理论一致性保证，无法确保不确定性质量，这严重限制了CNN在对预测不确定性要求极高的领域（如医学）中的应用。

Method: 提出了一种基于Bootstrap的新型预测不确定性估计框架。该方法的核心创新点在于：1）利用凸化神经网络建立Bootstrap的理论一致性证明；2）采用热启动策略避免每次Bootstrap从头拟合模型，显著降低计算负担；3）提出新型迁移学习方法，使框架能够应用于任意神经网络结构。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法相比其他基线CNN和最先进方法具有显著更优的性能。在多个图像数据集上的实验验证了所提框架的有效性，同时证明了其在计算效率上的显著优势。

Conclusion: 本研究成功填补了CNN不确定性量化领域的理论空白，提供了一种具有理论一致性保证的高效UQ方法。该框架不仅计算效率高，且具有广泛适用性，为CNN在医学等对预测不确定性要求严格领域的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Despite the popularity of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN), the problem of uncertainty quantification (UQ) of CNN has been largely overlooked. Lack of efficient UQ tools severely limits the application of CNN in certain areas, such as medicine, where prediction uncertainty is critically important. Among the few existing UQ approaches that have been proposed for deep learning, none of them has theoretical consistency that can guarantee the uncertainty quality. To address this issue, we propose a novel bootstrap based framework for the estimation of prediction uncertainty. The inference procedure we use relies on convexified neural networks to establish the theoretical consistency of bootstrap. Our approach has a significantly less computational load than its competitors, as it relies on warm-starts at each bootstrap that avoids refitting the model from scratch. We further explore a novel transfer learning method so our framework can work on arbitrary neural networks. We experimentally demonstrate our approach has a much better performance compared to other baseline CNNs and state-of-the-art methods on various image datasets.

</details>


### [102] [Schema-Adaptive Tabular Representation Learning with LLMs for Generalizable Multimodal Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11835)
*Hongxi Mao,Wei Zhou,Mengting Jia,Tao Fang,Huan Gao,Bin Zhang,Shangyang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Schema-Adaptive Tabular Representation Learning方法，利用大语言模型将结构化表格变量转换为语义自然语言陈述并编码，实现跨未知模式的零样本对齐，在痴呆症诊断任务中结合MRI数据达到SOTA性能，表现优于临床专家基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据的机器学习受限于模式泛化能力差，根本原因在于缺乏对结构化变量的语义理解。这一问题在临床医学等电子健康记录（EHR）模式差异显著的领域尤为突出，需要一种能够处理异构模式的方法。

Method: 提出Schema-Adaptive Tabular Representation Learning方法：1）将结构化变量转换为语义自然语言陈述；2）使用预训练大语言模型（LLM）对这些陈述进行编码，创建可迁移的表格嵌入；3）实现跨未知模式的零样本对齐，无需人工特征工程或重新训练；4）将该编码器集成到多模态框架中，结合表格数据和MRI数据进行痴呆症诊断。

Result: 在NACC和ADNI数据集上的实验表明：1）达到最先进（SOTA）性能；2）成功实现向未知模式的零样本迁移；3）在回顾性诊断任务中显著优于临床基线方法，包括委员会认证的神经科医师；4）验证了该方法在异构真实世界数据中的可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的方法为异构真实世界数据提供了可扩展、鲁棒的解决方案，为将基于LLM的推理扩展到结构化领域开辟了路径。该方法有效解决了表格数据模式泛化的核心挑战，具有广泛的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Machine learning for tabular data remains constrained by poor schema generalization, a challenge rooted in the lack of semantic understanding of structured variables. This challenge is particularly acute in domains like clinical medicine, where electronic health record (EHR) schemas vary significantly. To solve this problem, we propose Schema-Adaptive Tabular Representation Learning, a novel method that leverages large language models (LLMs) to create transferable tabular embeddings. By transforming structured variables into semantic natural language statements and encoding them with a pretrained LLM, our approach enables zero-shot alignment across unseen schemas without manual feature engineering or retraining. We integrate our encoder into a multimodal framework for dementia diagnosis, combining tabular and MRI data. Experiments on NACC and ADNI datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance and successful zero-shot transfer to unseen schemas, significantly outperforming clinical baselines, including board-certified neurologists, in retrospective diagnostic tasks. These results validate our LLM-driven approach as a scalable, robust solution for heterogeneous real-world data, offering a pathway to extend LLM-based reasoning to structured domains.

</details>


### [103] [AutoSurrogate: An LLM-Driven Multi-Agent Framework for Autonomous Construction of Deep Learning Surrogate Models in Subsurface Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11945)
*Jiale Liu,Nanzhe Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AutoSurrogate，一个由大语言模型驱动的多智能体框架，使非机器学习专家能够通过自然语言指令构建地下流动问题的高质量代理模型。在3D地质碳储存建模任务中，无需手动调优即可超越专家设计的基线和领域无关的AutoML方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下流动的高保真数值模拟计算量大，尤其是在不确定性量化和数据同化等多查询任务中。深度学习代理模型可以显著加速模拟，但构建这些模型需要大量机器学习专业知识（架构设计、超参数调优等），而大多数领域科学家缺乏这些知识。此外，该过程主要依赖人工和启发式选择，这种专业知识差距是DL代理技术更广泛应用的关键障碍。

Method: 开发了一个基于大语言模型的多智能体框架AutoSurrogate。该框架包含四个专门化智能体，协同执行数据剖析、从模型库中选择架构、贝叶斯超参数优化、模型训练以及根据用户指定阈值进行质量评估。系统能够自主处理常见失败模式，包括在数值不稳定时以调整后的配置重新训练，以及在预测精度未达目标时切换到替代架构。

Result: 在3D地质碳储存建模任务中进行了验证，将渗透率场映射到31个时间步的压力场和CO₂饱和度场。AutoSurrogate能够在无需任何手动调优的情况下，超越专家设计的基线方法和领域无关的AutoML方法，证明了其在实际部署中的强大潜力。

Conclusion: AutoSurrogate成功弥合了领域科学家与机器学习专业知识之间的鸿沟，使得非专家用户仅需单句自然语言指令即可生成可部署的代理模型，大幅降低了深度学习代理模型在科学计算领域的应用门槛，同时保证了高质量的预测性能。

Abstract: High-fidelity numerical simulation of subsurface flow is computationally intensive, especially for many-query tasks such as uncertainty quantification and data assimilation. Deep learning (DL) surrogates can significantly accelerate forward simulations, yet constructing them requires substantial machine learning (ML) expertise - from architecture design to hyperparameter tuning - that most domain scientists do not possess. Furthermore, the process is predominantly manual and relies heavily on heuristic choices. This expertise gap remains a key barrier to the broader adoption of DL surrogate techniques. For this reason, we present AutoSurrogate, a large-language-model-driven multi-agent framework that enables practitioners without ML expertise to build high-quality surrogates for subsurface flow problems through natural-language instructions. Given simulation data and optional preferences, four specialized agents collaboratively execute data profiling, architecture selection from a model zoo, Bayesian hyperparameter optimization, model training, and quality assessment against user-specified thresholds. The system also handles common failure modes autonomously, including restarting training with adjusted configurations when numerical instabilities occur and switching to alternative architectures when predictive accuracy falls short of targets. In our setting, a single natural-language sentence can be sufficient to produce a deployment-ready surrogate model, with minimum human intervention required at any intermediate stage. We demonstrate the utility of AutoSurrogate on a 3D geological carbon storage modeling task, mapping permeability fields to pressure and CO$_2$ saturation fields over 31 timesteps. Without any manual tuning, AutoSurrogate is able to outperform expert-designed baselines and domain-agnostic AutoML methods, demonstrating strong potential for practical deployment.

</details>


### [104] [A Layer-wise Analysis of Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11838)
*Qinghua Zhao,Xueling Gong,Xinyu Chen,Zhongfeng Kang,Xinlu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现指令遵循能力在模型中间层（20%-80%）呈现稳定涌现，提出Mid-Block Efficient Tuning方法，在GSM8K上比LoRA提升10.2%，证明有效对齐在架构上是局部化的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管监督微调（SFT）对模型对齐至关重要，但存在灾难性遗忘风险，且指令遵循能力在模型各层中如何涌现的机制尚不明确。现有研究缺乏对SFT过程中层级行为变化的深入理解。

Method: 跨模型规模（1B-32B）综合运用信息论、几何和优化指标进行系统性分析，发现中间层（20%-80%）稳定而末层高敏感的深度依赖模式。基于此提出Mid-Block Efficient Tuning方法，选择性更新关键中间层参数。

Result: 实验揭示了明确的深度依赖模式：中间层稳定，末层高敏感。所提方法在GSM8K数据集上（OLMo2-7B）性能超越标准LoRA达10.2%，同时降低参数开销。

Conclusion: 有效的模型对齐在架构上呈现局部化特征而非分布式，通过针对性更新中间层即可实现高效对齐，为SFT策略优化提供了新的理论依据和实践方案。

Abstract: While critical for alignment, Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) incurs the risk of catastrophic forgetting, yet the layer-wise emergence of instruction-following capabilities remains elusive. We investigate this mechanism via a comprehensive analysis utilizing information-theoretic, geometric, and optimization metrics across model scales (1B-32B). Our experiments reveal a distinct depth-dependent pattern: middle layers (20\%-80\%) are stable, whereas final layers exhibit high sensitivity. Leveraging this insight, we propose Mid-Block Efficient Tuning, which selectively updates these critical intermediate layers. Empirically, our method outperforms standard LoRA up to 10.2\% on GSM8K (OLMo2-7B) with reduced parameter overhead, demonstrating that effective alignment is architecturally localized rather than distributed. The code is publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/base_sft.

</details>


### [105] [Active Imitation Learning for Thermal- and Kernel-Aware LFM Inference on 3D S-NUCA Many-Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11948)
*Yixian Shen,Chaoyao Shen,Jan Deen,George Floros,Andy Pimentel,Anuj Pathania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AILFM框架，基于主动模仿学习在大规模基础模型推理中实现热感知调度，在3D S-NUCA架构上平衡性能与热安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模基础模型（LFM）推理在传统GPU上面临可用性受限和成本高昂的问题，促使研究者转向高性能通用CPU，特别是新兴的3D堆叠静态非均匀缓存架构（3D S-NUCA）系统。然而，这类架构虽然提供了更高的带宽和局部性，却面临严峻的热挑战，且由于3D片上网络导致缓存延迟不均匀。现有热管理方法依赖过度简化的分析模型，缺乏适应性，而LFM内核多样性和系统异构性使得线程迁移和电压/频率（V/f）缩放的最优管理变得非常复杂。

Method: 提出AILFM，一种基于主动模仿学习（AIL）的调度框架。该方法从Oracle演示中学习近似最优的热感知调度策略，具有最小的运行时开销。AILFM同时考虑核心级性能异构性和LFM中特定内核的行为特征，在保持热安全的同时最大化性能表现。

Result: 大量实验表明，AILFM优于最先进的基线方法，并且在多样化的LFM工作负载中表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: AILFM为解决3D S-NUCA架构上LFM推理的热管理问题提供了有效解决方案，证明了基于主动模仿学习的调度方法在处理复杂异构系统和多样化工作负载方面的优势，为未来CPU上高效运行大规模基础模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Foundation Model (LFM) inference is both memory- and compute-intensive, traditionally relying on GPUs. However, the limited availability and high cost have motivated the adoption of high-performance general-purpose CPUs, especially emerging 3D-stacked Static Non-Uniform Cache Architecture (3D S-NUCA) systems. These architectures offer enhanced bandwidth and locality but suffer from severe thermal challenges and uneven cache latencies due to 3D Networks-on-Chip (NoC). Optimal management of thread migration and V/f scaling is non-trivial due to LFM kernel diversity and system heterogeneity. Existing thermal management approaches often rely on oversimplified analytical models and lack adaptability. We propose AILFM, an Active Imitation Learning (AIL)-based scheduling framework that learns near-optimal thermal-aware scheduling policies from Oracle demonstrations with minimal run-time overhead. AILFM accounts for both core-level performance heterogeneity and kernel-specific behavior in LFMs to maintain thermal safety while maximizing performance. Extensive experiments show that AILFM outperforms state-of-the-art baselines and generalizes well across diverse LFM workloads.

</details>


### [106] [When Reasoning Models Hurt Behavioral Simulation: A Solver-Sampler Mismatch in Multi-Agent LLM Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11840)
*Sandro Andric*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现更强的推理能力未必提升多智能体模拟的真实性。在需要模拟有限理性行为的场景中，推理增强模型倾向于过度优化策略主导行为而牺牲妥协结果。受限反思机制在三个谈判实验中成功生成更多样化、更符合现实的妥协轨迹，而GPT-5.2原生推理在45次实验中全部导向权威决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前普遍假设更强的推理能力会提升模拟真实性，但该假设在非策略优化目标（如采样合理的有限理性行为）场景下可能失效。研究者发现推理增强模型可能成为更好的问题求解者却是更差的模拟器，存在求解器-采样器失配问题。

Method: 在三个多智能体谈判环境中进行实验：模糊分权交易限制场景、模糊统一反对交易限制场景、以及新领域电网紧急限电案例。比较三种反思条件（无反思、受限反思、原生推理），横跨两个主要模型家族，并扩展测试OpenAI的GPT-4.1和GPT-5.2。

Result: 受限反思在所有三个实验中均产生比无反思或原生推理更丰富、更偏向妥协的轨迹。在OpenAI扩展实验中，GPT-5.2原生推理在45次运行中全部导向权威决策，而GPT-5.2受限反思在每个环境中都恢复了妥协结果。

Conclusion: 模型能力与模拟真实性是不同目标，行为模拟应将模型视为采样器而非仅作为求解器来评估。这是方法论警示：更强推理能力并非普遍有益，需要根据模拟目标选择合适的推理配置。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as agents in social, economic, and policy simulations. A common assumption is that stronger reasoning should improve simulation fidelity. We argue that this assumption can fail when the objective is not to solve a strategic problem, but to sample plausible boundedly rational behavior. In such settings, reasoning-enhanced models can become better solvers and worse simulators: they can over-optimize for strategically dominant actions, collapse compromise-oriented terminal behavior, and sometimes exhibit a diversity-without-fidelity pattern in which local variation survives without outcome-level fidelity. We study this solver-sampler mismatch in three multi-agent negotiation environments adapted from earlier simulation work: an ambiguous fragmented-authority trading-limits scenario, an ambiguous unified-opposition trading-limits scenario, and a new-domain grid-curtailment case in emergency electricity management. We compare three reflection conditions, no reflection, bounded reflection, and native reasoning, across two primary model families and then extend the same protocol to direct OpenAI runs with GPT-4.1 and GPT-5.2. Across all three experiments, bounded reflection produces substantially more diverse and compromise-oriented trajectories than either no reflection or native reasoning. In the direct OpenAI extension, GPT-5.2 native ends in authority decisions in 45 of 45 runs across the three experiments, while GPT-5.2 bounded recovers compromise outcomes in every environment. The contribution is not a claim that reasoning is generally harmful. It is a methodological warning: model capability and simulation fidelity are different objectives, and behavioral simulation should qualify models as samplers, not only as solvers.

</details>


### [107] [Polynomial Expansion Rank Adaptation: Enhancing Low-Rank Fine-Tuning with High-Order Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11841)
*Wenhao Zhang,Lin Mu,Li Ni,Peiquan Jin,Yiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PERA方法，通过在低秩因子空间引入结构化多项式扩展，解决了LoRA线性结构表达能力受限的问题，在不增加秩或推理成本的情况下实现更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩适应(LoRA)广泛应用于大语言模型的高效微调，但其严格的线性结构从根本上限制了表达能力。其双线性权重更新公式仅能捕获低秩因子间的一阶依赖关系，限制了对非线性和高阶参数交互的建模能力。

Method: 提出多项式扩展秩适应(PERA)方法，直接在低秩因子空间引入结构化多项式扩展。通过扩展每个低秩因子以在组合前合成高阶交互项，将适应空间转换为能够建模更丰富非线性耦合的多项式流形，且不增加秩或推理成本。

Result: 理论分析表明PERA相比现有线性适应方法具有更强的表达能力和更有效的特征利用。实验上，PERA在多个基准测试中持续优于最先进方法。特别是实验表明，引入高阶非线性分量（尤其是平方项）对于增强表达能力和在各种秩设置下保持强劲稳健的性能至关重要。

Conclusion: PERA通过多项式扩展成功突破了LoRA线性结构的表达瓶颈，证明了高阶非线性分量的重要性，为大语言模型的高效微调提供了新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is a widely used strategy for efficient fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs), but its strictly linear structure fundamentally limits expressive capacity. The bilinear formulation of weight updates captures only first-order dependencies between low-rank factors, restricting the modeling of nonlinear and higher-order parameter interactions. In this paper, we propose Polynomial Expansion Rank Adaptation (PERA), a novel method that introduces structured polynomial expansion directly into the low-rank factor space. By expanding each low-rank factor to synthesize high-order interaction terms before composition, PERA transforms the adaptation space into a polynomial manifold capable of modeling richer nonlinear coupling without increasing rank or inference cost. We provide theoretical analysis demonstrating that PERA offers enhanced expressive capacity and more effective feature utilization compare to existing linear adaptation approaches. Empirically, PERA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across diverse benchmarks. Notably, our experiments show that incorporating high-order nonlinear components particularly square terms is crucial for enhancing expressive capacity and maintaining strong and robust performance under various rank settings. Our code is available at https://github.com/zhangwenhao6/PERA

</details>


### [108] [TCL: Enabling Fast and Efficient Cross-Hardware Tensor Program Optimization via Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12891)
*Chaoyao Shen,Linfeng Jiang,Yixian Shen,Tao Xu,Guoqing Li,Anuj Pathania,Andy D. Pimentel,Meng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TCL框架，通过高效采样、Mamba代价模型和连续知识蒸馏，在CPU和GPU上分别实现16.8倍和12.48倍加速，降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习编译器依赖大规模离线数据集进行代价建模和自动调优，导致数据收集成本高昂且跨平台迁移性差。

Method: 提出TCL框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) RDU Sampler主动学习策略，通过联合优化代表性、多样性和不确定性仅选择10%的张量程序；(2) 基于Mamba的代价模型，以轻量化序列建模捕捉长距离调度依赖；(3) 连续知识蒸馏框架，跨多硬件平台逐步迁移知识，避免传统多任务学习的参数爆炸问题。

Result: 实验表明，TCL在CPU和GPU平台上相比Tenset-MLP平均调优时间分别加速16.8倍和12.48倍，推理延迟分别降低1.20倍和1.13倍。

Conclusion: TCL框架有效解决了现有编译器的数据依赖和迁移性问题，在保持模型精度的同时大幅提升调优效率，为跨平台张量程序优化提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning (DL) compilers rely on cost models and auto-tuning to optimize tensor programs for target hardware. However, existing approaches depend on large offline datasets, incurring high collection costs and offering suboptimal transferability across platforms. In this paper, we introduce TCL, a novel efficient and transferable compiler framework for fast tensor program optimization across diverse hardware platforms to address these challenges. Specifically, TCL is built on three core enablers: (1) the RDU Sampler, a data-efficient active learning strategy that selects only 10% of tensor programs by jointly optimizing Representativeness, Diversity, and Uncertainty, substantially reducing data collection costs while maintaining near-original model accuracy; (2) a new Mamba-based cost model that efficiently captures long-range schedule dependencies while achieving a favorable trade-off between prediction accuracy and computational cost through reduced parameterization and lightweight sequence modeling; and (3) a continuous knowledge distillation framework that effectively and progressively transfers knowledge across multiple hardware platforms while avoiding the parameter explosion and data dependency issues typically caused by traditional multi-task learning. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of each individual enabler and the holistic TCL framework. When optimizing a range of mainstream DL models on both CPU and GPU platforms, TCL achieves, on average, 16.8x and 12.48x faster tuning time, and 1.20x and 1.13x lower inference latency, respectively, compared to Tenset-MLP.

</details>


### [109] [DBGL: Decay-aware Bipartite Graph Learning for Irregular Medical Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11842)
*Jian Chen,Yuzhu Hu,Xiaoyan Yuan,Yuxuan Hu,Jinfeng Xu,Yipeng Du,Wenhao Yuan,Wei Wang,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DBGL模型，通过 Decay-Aware Bipartite Graph Learning 解决不规则医疗时间序列建模问题，在四个公开数据集上超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则医疗时间序列因异构采样率、异步观测和可变间隔而具有固有的不规则性。现有方法在建模时往往扭曲了时间采样的不规则性和缺失模式，且未能捕捉变量的衰减不规则性，导致表示学习效果次优。

Method: 提出DBGL（Decay-Aware Bipartite Graph Learning）方法：1) 构建患者-变量二部图，在不进行人工对齐的情况下同时捕捉不规则采样模式，自适应建模变量关系；2) 设计节点特定的时间衰减编码机制，基于采样间隔捕捉每个变量的衰减率，精确表征不规则时间动态。

Result: 在四个公开可用的数据集上进行了评估，DBGL的性能超越了所有基线方法。

Conclusion: DBGL有效解决了不规则医疗时间序列建模中的关键挑战，能够更好地保留时间采样的不规则性和缺失模式，同时捕捉变量衰减不规则性，显著提升了患者状态表示学习的质量。

Abstract: Irregular Medical Time Series play a critical role in the clinical domain to better understand the patient's condition. However, inherent irregularity arising from heterogeneous sampling rates, asynchronous observations, and variable gaps poses key challenges for reliable modeling. Existing methods often distort temporal sampling irregularity and missingness patterns while failing to capture variable decay irregularity, resulting in suboptimal representations. To address these limitations, we introduce DBGL, Decay-Aware Bipartite Graph Learning for Irregular Medical Time Series. DBGL first introduces a patient-variable bipartite graph that simultaneously captures irregular sampling patterns without artificial alignment and adaptively models variable relationships for temporal sampling irregularity modeling, enhancing representation learning. To model variable decay irregularity, DBGL designs a novel node-specific temporal decay encoding mechanism that captures each variable's decay rates based on sampling interval, yielding a more accurate and faithful representation of irregular temporal dynamics. We evaluate the performance of DBGL on four publicly available datasets, and the results show that DBGL outperforms all baselines.

</details>


### [110] [Disposition Distillation at Small Scale: A Three-Arc Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11867)
*Hari Sadasivan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇负面结果论文，旨在通过四阶段蒸馏流水线向小型语言模型（0.6B至2.3B参数）注入行为倾向（自我验证、不确定性确认、反馈整合），但初始报告的显著性能提升（MCAS +33.9分、HumanEval +15.3分）在二次验证中被证伪（分别为评分方式不当和截断伪影）。后续在五个模型家族、三种技术路径（SFT/DPO LoRA、推理时注意力头干预、冻结基座置信度门控边车）上进行的系统实验均表明：不存在能在不损害内容质量或不退化为风格模仿的前提下，有效改变模型行为倾向的操作符。研究提出了一个诚实的证伪流水线，将假阳性转化为可发表的负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机是探索是否可以通过训练和干预手段，将特定的行为倾向（如自我验证、不确定性确认和反馈整合）注入到小型语言模型中，以提升模型的可靠性和可信度。研究团队最初希望开发一套有效的技术方案，但在发现初始实验结果存在错误后，转向系统性地验证和报告负面结果，以避免学术界产生类似的假阳性发现。

Method: 研究采用三弧线技术路径进行系统性验证：（1）在三个模型家族和两个领域上进行SFT/DPO LoRA训练；（2）推理时对o_proj进行注意力头干预（tempering）；（3）训练无关的冻结基座边车机制，读取最终token隐藏状态h_last。实验覆盖五个小型模型（Qwen3-0.6B、Qwen3-1.7B、Qwen3.5-0.8B、Gemma 4 E2B、SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct），并通过跨分布验证评估泛化能力。研究还建立了诚实的证伪流水线，包括对初始报告结果的二次完整性检查。

Result: 核心结果为负面发现：三种技术路径均无法在保持内容质量的前提下改变模型的评判测量行为倾向。所有干预手段要么损害内容质量，要么退化为风格模仿。跨分布验证显示泛化失败：域内交叉验证AUC=0.683，在新提示上降至随机水平（AUC=0.516）。此外，发现Gemma 4 E2B在Chef领域存在置信度-正确性近乎完全解耦的现象（断言不对称性-0.009，模型在91%的情况下做出断言，与正确性无关）。研究还提出了针对线性h_last探针的双失败模式分类体系。

Conclusion: 研究得出结论：当前技术手段无法在不损害模型核心能力的前提下，有效注入或改变小型语言模型的行为倾向。这一负面结果具有方法学价值，展示了如何通过严格的证伪流水线将假阳性发现转化为可发表的负面结果，为后续研究提供了警示和方法学参考。研究强调了在报告行为干预效果时进行严格跨分布验证的重要性。

Abstract: We set out to train behavioral dispositions (self-verification, uncertainty acknowledgment, feedback integration) into small language models (0.6B to 2.3B effective parameters) through a four-stage all-MIT distillation pipeline, with follow-on experiments on inference-time attention-head interventions and a frozen-base confidence-gated sidecar. An internal draft reported +33.9-point MCAS and +15.3-point HumanEval gains on a Qwen3-0.6B student; a second-pass sanity check falsified both numbers before publication. The HumanEval delta was a truncation artifact (n_predict=512) that inverted to -8.0 points at n_predict=1024; the MCAS gain disappeared under apples-to-apples scoring. That falsification triggered three subsequent arcs. Across (1) SFT/DPO LoRA on three model families and two domains, (2) inference-time attention-head tempering on o_proj, and (3) a training-free frozen-base sidecar reading the final-token hidden state h_last, we find no operator that moves judge-measured disposition without damaging content or collapsing into stylistic mimicry. The failure is consistent across five models (Qwen3-0.6B, Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3.5-0.8B, Gemma 4 E2B, and SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct). A within-distribution cross-validation pass (AUC=0.683) collapsed to chance on fresh prompts (AUC=0.516). We contribute a three-arc negative result with mechanism, a two-failure-mode taxonomy for linear h_last probes, and an honest falsification pipeline that converts the class of false positives we ourselves produced into publishable negatives. As an independent finding, Gemma 4 E2B exhibits near-complete confidence-correctness decoupling on the Chef domain (assertion asymmetry -0.009; the model asserts at 91% regardless of correctness).

</details>


### [111] [Subcritical Signal Propagation at Initialization in Normalization-Free Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11890)
*Sergey Alekseev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了平均部分雅可比范数（APJN）来研究Transformer初始化时的信号传播，发现Pre-LN架构呈幂律增长（临界态），而DyT/Derf等非线性替换架构呈拉伸指数增长（亚临界态），解释了后者训练不稳定的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer初始化时的信号传播与梯度放大问题，特别是针对双向注意力机制的Vision Transformer及使用动态激活函数（DyT、Derf）的变体架构，分析其训练稳定性与初始化敏感性的理论基础。

Method: 提出平均部分雅可比范数（APJN）作为衡量跨层梯度放大的指标；针对双向注意力与置换对称输入配置，推导激活统计量与APJN在各层间的递推关系；通过理论分析预测APJN在大深度下的渐近行为，并在深度Vision Transformer上进行实验验证。

Result: 1）理论预测的APJN渐近行为与实测值在深度Vision Transformer上吻合；2）Pre-LN架构的APJN呈幂律增长（临界态）；3）使用类tanh非线性替换LayerNorm的架构，APJN呈拉伸指数增长（亚临界态）；4）理论成功解释了DyT与Derf Transformer对初始化和优化更敏感、需精细调参的原因。

Conclusion: ResNet中的临界性理论可推广至Transformer：Pre-LN处于临界态，而DyT/Derf等架构处于亚临界态。这为理解不同Transformer架构的信号传播特性与训练稳定性提供了统一的理论框架与设计指导。

Abstract: We study signal propagation at initialization in transformers through the averaged partial Jacobian norm (APJN), a measure of gradient amplification across layers. We extend APJN analysis to transformers with bidirectional attention and permutation-symmetric input token configurations by deriving recurrence relations for activation statistics and APJNs across layers. Our theory predicts how attention modifies the asymptotic behavior of the APJN at large depth and matches APJNs measured in deep vision transformers. The criticality picture known from residual networks carries over to transformers: the pre-LayerNorm architecture exhibits power-law APJN growth, whereas transformers with LayerNorm replaced by elementwise $\tanh$-like nonlinearities have stretched-exponential APJN growth, indicating that the latter are subcritical. Applied to Dynamic Tanh (DyT) and Dynamic erf (Derf) transformers, the theory explains why these architectures can be more sensitive to initialization and optimization choices and require careful tuning for stable training.

</details>


### [112] [Thermodynamic Liquid Manifold Networks: Physics-Bounded Deep Learning for Solar Forecasting in Autonomous Off-Grid Microgrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11909)
*Mohammed Ezzaldin Babiker Abdullah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出热力学液态流形网络(TLMN)，通过将气象变量投影到Koopman线性化黎曼流形，解决了深度学习光伏预测模型中的相位滞后和夜间幻象发电问题，实现零夜间误差和快速响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习光伏预测模型存在严重缺陷：云层瞬变时产生时间相位滞后，以及物理上不可能的夜间幻象发电现象。这些数据驱动模型与确定性天体力学之间存在分歧，影响了离网光伏系统的稳定运行。

Method: 提出热力学液态流形网络(TLMN)：将22个气象和几何变量投影到Koopman线性化黎曼流形；集成光谱校准单元和乘性热力学Alpha门；将实时大气不透明度与理论晴空边界模型合成，从结构上强制遵守天体几何约束。

Result: 在恶劣半干旱气候下五年测试周期验证：RMSE为18.31 Wh/m²，皮尔逊相关系数0.988；所有1826个测试日严格保持零夜间误差；高频光学瞬变期间相位响应小于30分钟。模型仅含63,458个可训练参数，为超轻量级设计。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了稳健、热力学一致的标准，适用于边缘可部署微电网控制器，成功解决了数据驱动建模与天体力学之间的分歧问题。

Abstract: The stable operation of autonomous off-grid photovoltaic systems requires solar forecasting algorithms that respect atmospheric thermodynamics. Contemporary deep learning models consistently exhibit critical anomalies, primarily severe temporal phase lags during cloud transients and physically impossible nocturnal power generation. To resolve this divergence between data-driven modeling and deterministic celestial mechanics, this research introduces the Thermodynamic Liquid Manifold Network. The methodology projects 22 meteorological and geometric variables into a Koopman-linearized Riemannian manifold to systematically map complex climatic dynamics. The architecture integrates a Spectral Calibration unit and a multiplicative Thermodynamic Alpha-Gate. This system synthesizes real-time atmospheric opacity with theoretical clear-sky boundary models, structurally enforcing strict celestial geometry compliance. This completely neutralizes phantom nocturnal generation while maintaining zero-lag synchronization during rapid weather shifts. Validated against a rigorous five-year testing horizon in a severe semi-arid climate, the framework achieves an RMSE of 18.31 Wh/m2 and a Pearson correlation of 0.988. The model strictly maintains a zero-magnitude nocturnal error across all 1826 testing days and exhibits a sub-30-minute phase response during high-frequency optical transients. Comprising exactly 63,458 trainable parameters, this ultra-lightweight design establishes a robust, thermodynamically consistent standard for edge-deployable microgrid controllers.

</details>


### [113] [How Transformers Learn to Plan via Multi-Token Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11912)
*Jianhao Huang,Zhanpeng Zhou,Renqiu Xia,Baharan Mirzasoleiman,Weijie Su,Wei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论和实证两个角度证明了多token预测（MTP）通过引入梯度解耦特性，诱导模型形成逆向推理过程，从而在规划和推理任务上显著优于传统的下一token预测（NTP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的下一token预测（NTP）在推理任务中难以捕捉全局结构。虽然多token预测（MTP）已成为一种有潜力的替代方案，但其底层工作机制尚不明确，研究旨在探究MTP如何促进推理，特别是规划能力。

Method: 通过在合成图寻径任务及倒计时（Countdown）、布尔可满足性问题等现实推理基准上进行实证对比；理论上，针对星图任务分析简化两层Transformer的机制，证明MTP诱导出“先关注终点节点，再反向重构路径”的两阶段逆向推理过程。

Result: MTP在合成图寻径任务和现实推理基准上均一致优于NTP；理论分析表明MTP具有梯度解耦特性，相比NTP提供了更清晰的训练信号，从而产生可解释的逆向推理电路。

Conclusion: 多token目标函数本质上将优化偏向于稳健且可解释的推理电路，揭示了MTP促进推理的内在机制，为语言模型训练目标的设计提供了新见解。

Abstract: While next-token prediction (NTP) has been the standard objective for training language models, it often struggles to capture global structure in reasoning tasks. Multi-token prediction (MTP) has recently emerged as a promising alternative, yet its underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this paper, we study how MTP facilitates reasoning, with a focus on planning. Empirically, we show that MTP consistently outperforms NTP on both synthetic graph path-finding tasks and more realistic reasoning benchmarks, such as Countdown and boolean satisfiability problems. Theoretically, we analyze a simplified two-layer Transformer on a star graph task. We prove that MTP induces a two-stage reverse reasoning process: the model first attends to the end node and then reconstructs the path by tracing intermediate nodes backward. This behavior arises from a gradient decoupling property of MTP, which provides a cleaner training signal compared to NTP. Ultimately, our results highlight how multi-token objectives inherently bias optimization toward robust and interpretable reasoning circuits.

</details>


### [114] [Can AI Detect Life? Lessons from Artificial Life](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11915)
*Ankit Gupta,Christoph Adami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 机器学习方法在地外生命探测中存在严重的假阳性问题，容易被分布外样本欺骗，导致即使样本不具生命特征也会以近100%的置信度检测到生命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习方法已被提议用于检测地外样本中的生命，基于其在区分自然和合成有机分子混合物训练模型上的能力。然而，地外样本很可能超出了地球生物和非生物样本的分布范围，这可能导致机器学习方法产生显著的假阳性结果。

Method: 使用人工生命（Artificial Life）方法进行实验验证，测试现代机器学习方法在分析不具备生命能力的样本时的表现，验证机器学习方法是否容易被分布外样本所欺骗。

Result: 实验证明，现代机器学习方法很容易被欺骗，即使分析样本不具备生命能力，也会以接近100%的置信度检测到生命。这是由于机器学习方法容易被分布外样本所欺骗的特性所致。

Conclusion: 由于地外样本很可能超出地球生物和非生物样本的分布范围，使用AI方法进行生命探测必然会产生显著的假阳性结果，这表明在地外生命探测中直接应用机器学习方法存在根本性的局限性。

Abstract: Modern machine learning methods have been proposed to detect life in extraterrestrial samples, drawing on their ability to distinguish biotic from abiotic samples based on training models using natural and synthetic organic molecular mixtures. Here we show using Artificial Life that such methods are easily fooled into detecting life with near 100% confidence even if the analyzed sample is not capable of life. This is due to modern machine learning methods' propensity to be easily fooled by out-of-distribution samples. Because extra-terrestrial samples are very likely out of the distribution provided by terrestrial biotic and abiotic samples, using AI methods for life detection is bound to yield significant false positives.

</details>


### [115] [INTARG: Informed Real-Time Adversarial Attack Generation for Time-Series Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11928)
*Gamze Kirman Tokgoz,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing,Baris Aksanli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对时间序列预测的在线对抗攻击框架，通过选择性攻击策略，在攻击少于10%的时间步长下，将预测误差提高了2.42倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习模型在时间序列预测中应用广泛，但其易受对抗攻击影响。现有的先进攻击方法在时间序列场景中并不实用，因为存储完整历史数据或在每一步都进行攻击往往不可行。

Method: 提出了一种在线有界缓冲区设置下的对抗攻击框架，采用知情选择性攻击策略，专门针对模型表现出高置信度且预期预测误差最大的时间步长进行攻击。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架能够将预测误差提高至2.42倍，同时仅在少于10%的时间步长上执行攻击。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在时间序列预测中，通过明智地选择攻击时机，可以实现更高效且更具破坏性的对抗攻击，为评估时间序列模型的鲁棒性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting aims to predict future values by modeling temporal dependencies in historical observations. It is a critical component of many real-world systems, where accurate forecasts improve operational efficiency and help mitigate uncertainty and risk. More recently, machine learning (ML), and especially deep learning (DL)-based models, have gained widespread adoption for time-series forecasting, but they remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks. However, many state-of-the-art attack methods are not directly applicable in time-series settings, where storing complete historical data or performing attacks at every time step is often impractical. This paper proposes an adversarial attack framework for time-series forecasting under an online bounded-buffer setting, leveraging an informed and selective attack strategy. By selectively targeting time steps where the model exhibits high confidence and the expected prediction error is maximal, our framework produces fewer but substantially more effective attacks. Experiments show that our framework can increase the prediction error up to 2.42x, while performing attacks in fewer than 10% of time steps.

</details>


### [116] [Fast and principled equation discovery from chaos to climate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11929)
*Yuzheng Zhang,Weizhen Li,Rui Carvalho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Bayesian-ARGOS框架，通过结合频率论筛选与贝叶斯推断，实现了在稀疏噪声数据下高效、自动化的方程发现与不确定性量化，在多个混沌系统中性能优于现有方法，计算成本降低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从噪声大、数据稀缺的观测中直接发现动力学方程是数据驱动科学的核心挑战。现有的基于库的稀疏回归方法在自动化、统计严谨性和计算效率之间存在权衡，难以同时满足，因此迫切需要一种能兼顾这些需求的新方法。

Method: 开发了一种混合框架Bayesian-ARGOS，首先利用快速的频率论方法进行筛选，随后进行聚焦的贝叶斯推断，从而在保证自动化的同时提供了有原则的不确定性量化，并显著降低了计算成本。

Result: 在七个混沌系统的测试中，Bayesian-ARGOS在大多数场景下优于两种先进方法。相比SINDy，在所有系统中具有更高的数据效率，在七个系统中的六个上具有更强的噪声容忍度；与基于自举的ARGOS相比，计算成本降低了两个数量级。此外，概率模型支持影响分析和多重共线性检测等标准统计诊断。在与SINDy-SHRED结合用于海表温度重建时，显著提高了有效潜变量方程的产率和长期稳定性。

Conclusion: Bayesian-ARGOS为从稀缺和噪声观测中发现可解释的控制方程提供了一条有原则、自动化且计算高效的途径，为从基准混沌系统到全球气候模式背后的潜在动力学等跨尺度方程发现提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Our ability to predict, control, and ultimately understand complex systems rests on discovering the equations that govern their dynamics. Identifying these equations directly from noisy, limited observations has therefore become a central challenge in data-driven science, yet existing library-based sparse regression methods force a compromise between automation, statistical rigor, and computational efficiency. Here we develop Bayesian-ARGOS, a hybrid framework that reconciles these demands by combining rapid frequentist screening with focused Bayesian inference, enabling automated equation discovery with principled uncertainty quantification at a fraction of the computational cost of existing methods. Tested on seven chaotic systems under varying data scarcity and noise levels, Bayesian-ARGOS outperforms two state-of-the-art methods in most scenarios. It surpasses SINDy in data efficiency for all systems and noise tolerance for six out of the seven, with a two-order-of-magnitude reduction in computational cost compared to bootstrap-based ARGOS. The probabilistic formulation additionally enables a suite of standard statistical diagnostics, including influence analysis and multicollinearity detection that expose failure modes otherwise opaque. When integrated with representation learning (SINDy-SHRED) for high dimensional sea surface temperature reconstruction, Bayesian-ARGOS increases the yield of valid latent equations with significantly improved long horizon stability. Bayesian-ARGOS thus provides a principled, automated, and computationally efficient route from scarce and noisy observations to interpretable governing equations, offering a practical framework for equation discovery across scales, from benchmark chaotic systems to the latent dynamics underlying global climate patterns.

</details>


### [117] [Multi-Worker Selection based Distributed Swarm Learning for Edge IoT with Non-i.i.d. Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18367)
*Zhuoyu Yao,Yue Wang,Songyang Zhang,Yingshu Li,Zhipeng Cai,Zhi Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式群体学习（DSL）在非独立同分布（non-i.i.d.）数据场景下的性能下降问题，提出了一种名为M-DSL的多工作者选择算法。通过引入新的非独立同分布度量指标来量化数据异构性，M-DSL能够有效选择对全局模型更新贡献突出的工作者，从而显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式群体学习在边缘物联网领域展现出巨大潜力，但非独立同分布数据的存在严重制约了其性能，导致学习效果下降和训练发散。此外，目前缺乏关于数据异构性如何影响模型训练准确性的理论指导，这一问题亟待深入研究。

Method: 本文提出了M-DSL算法。该方法首先引入并定义了一种新的非独立同分布度量指标，用于量化本地数据集之间的统计差异，从而建立数据异构性测量与DSL性能评估之间的联系。基于此度量，M-DSL指导有效地选择对全局模型更新做出突出贡献的多个工作者参与训练。

Result: 本文对M-DSL的收敛行为进行了理论分析，并在不同的异构数据集和非独立同分布数据设置上进行了大量实验。数值结果验证了M-DSL相比基准方法在性能提升和网络智能增强方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了DSL框架下数据异构性影响分析的空白，提出的M-DSL算法成功解决了非独立同分布数据带来的挑战，为边缘计算环境下的分布式学习提供了有效的解决方案和理论基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in distributed swarm learning (DSL) offer a promising paradigm for edge Internet of Things. Such advancements enhance data privacy, communication efficiency, energy saving, and model scalability. However, the presence of non-independent and identically distributed (non-i.i.d.) data pose a significant challenge for multi-access edge computing, degrading learning performance and diverging training behavior of vanilla DSL. Further, there still lacks theoretical guidance on how data heterogeneity affects model training accuracy, which requires thorough investigation. To fill the gap, this paper first study the data heterogeneity by measuring the impact of non-i.i.d. datasets under the DSL framework. This then motivates a new multi-worker selection design for DSL, termed M-DSL algorithm, which works effectively with distributed heterogeneous data. A new non-i.i.d. degree metric is introduced and defined in this work to formulate the statistical difference among local datasets, which builds a connection between the measure of data heterogeneity and the evaluation of DSL performance. In this way, our M-DSL guides effective selection of multiple works who make prominent contributions for global model updates. We also provide theoretical analysis on the convergence behavior of our M-DSL, followed by extensive experiments on different heterogeneous datasets and non-i.i.d. data settings. Numerical results verify performance improvement and network intelligence enhancement provided by our M-DSL beyond the benchmarks.

</details>


### [118] [A unified data format for managing diabetes time-series data: DIAbetes eXchange (DIAX)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11944)
*Elliott C. Pryor,Marc D. Breton,Anas El Fathi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DIAX（DIAbetes eXchange），一种基于JSON的标准化格式，用于统一糖尿病时间序列数据（包括CGM、胰岛素和餐食信号），以促进互操作性、可重复性和可扩展性，特别适用于机器学习应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病设备（包括连续血糖监测、智能胰岛素笔和自动胰岛素输送系统）生成丰富的时间序列数据，广泛应用于研究和机器学习。然而，不同数据源之间的格式不一致阻碍了数据共享、整合和分析。

Method: 开发了DIAX，一种基于JSON的标准化格式，用于统一糖尿病时间序列数据。提供了一个开源存储库，包含数据集转换、跨格式兼容性、可视化和社区贡献的工具。DIAX是一种转化资源而非数据托管平台，确保灵活性而不施加数据共享约束。

Result: DIAX目前与其他标准化工作兼容，并支持主要数据集（DCLP3、DCLP5、IOBP2、PEDAP、T1Dexi、Loop），总计超过1000万患者小时的数据。开源存储库已上线，提供相关工具。

Conclusion: DIAX通过提供标准化的数据格式和开源工具，有效解决了糖尿病时间序列数据格式不一致的问题，促进了数据互操作性和研究的可重复性，为机器学习在糖尿病领域的应用提供了重要基础设施支持。

Abstract: Diabetes devices, including Continuous Glucose Monitoring (CGM), Smart Insulin Pens, and Automated Insulin Delivery systems, generate rich time-series data widely used in research and machine learning. However, inconsistent data formats across sources hinder sharing, integration, and analysis.
  We present DIAX (DIAbetes eXchange), a standardized JSON-based format for unifying diabetes time-series data, including CGM, insulin, and meal signals. DIAX promotes interoperability, reproducibility, and extensibility, particularly for machine learning applications. An open-source repository provides tools for dataset conversion, cross-format compatibility, visualization, and community contributions. DIAX is a translational resource, not a data host, ensuring flexibility without imposing data-sharing constraints.
  Currently, DIAX is compatible with other standardization efforts and supports major datasets (DCLP3, DCLP5, IOBP2, PEDAP, T1Dexi, Loop), totaling over 10 million patient-hours of data. https://github.com/Center-for-Diabetes-Technology/DIAX

</details>


### [119] [ResBM: Residual Bottleneck Models for Low-Bandwidth Pipeline Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11947)
*Alan Aboudib,Rodrigo Lopez Portillo A.,Kalei Brady,Steffen Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ResBM架构，在低带宽去中心化环境下实现了128倍激活压缩，解决了流水线并行的高通信瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模去中心化训练面临低带宽通信瓶颈。虽然数据并行已有高效的去中心化方案，但流水线并行因需要传输大量激活值，在低带宽环境下仍然困难。现有方法如Subspace Models虽声称支持100倍压缩，但依赖复杂约束优化且无法实现真正的端到端训练。

Method: 提出Residual Bottleneck Model（ResBM）架构，从底层设计为原生适配低带宽通信环境。核心创新是在流水线边界引入残差编码器-解码器瓶颈模块，该模块可作为模型参数的一部分进行端到端训练，同时保留显式的低秩恒等路径。该设计可应用于任何标准Transformer架构。

Result: ResBM实现了128倍激活压缩，达到当前最优水平，且收敛速率无显著损失，内存和计算开销也可忽略不计。

Conclusion: ResBM成功解锁了大规模低带宽去中心化训练的潜力，能够利用原本被闲置的计算资源，为去中心化AI训练提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Unlocking large-scale low-bandwidth decentralized training has the potential to utilize otherwise untapped compute resources. In centralized settings, large-scale multi-node training is primarily enabled by data and pipeline parallelism, two techniques that require ultra-high-bandwidth communication. While efficient methods now exist for decentralized data parallelism, pipeline parallelism remains the primary challenge. Recent efforts, such as Subspace Models (SM), have claimed up to 100x activation compression but rely on complex constrained optimization and diverge from true end-to-end training. In this paper, we propose a different approach, based on an architecture designed from the ground up to be native to low-bandwidth communication environments while still applicable to any standard transformer-based architecture. We call this architecture the Residual Bottleneck Model or ResBM, it introduces a residual encoder-decoder bottleneck module across pipeline boundaries that can be trained end-to-end as part of the model's parameters while preserving an explicit low-rank identity path. We show that ResBMs achieve state-of-the-art 128x activation compression without significant loss in convergence rates and without significant memory or compute overhead.

</details>


### [120] [Decentralized Learning via Random Walk with Jumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12260)
*Zonghong Liu,Matthew Dwyer,Salim El Rouayheb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了加权随机游走学习中存在的“陷阱”现象，并提出了一种结合Levy跳跃的Metropolis-Hastings算法（MHLJ），通过引入偶发的长距离跳转来解决该问题，显著加速了去中心化学习的收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化网络学习中，加权随机游走通过设计转移矩阵来实现期望的采样分布以加速收敛。然而，作者发现使用Metropolis-Hastings算法实现加权采样时，可能会导致随机游走陷入网络的小区域（即“陷阱”），造成更新高度相关且收敛严重退化。这一未被探索的现象促使作者寻求解决方案。

Method: 作者提出了Metropolis-Hastings with Levy jumps (MHLJ) 算法。该方法在Metropolis-Hastings采样的基础上，引入了偶发的Levy跳跃，即在保持局部信息约束的同时，允许模型进行长距离的网络跳转，从而恢复对整个网络的探索能力，避免陷入局部区域。

Result: 研究建立了明确的收敛速率理论分析，揭示了数据异质性、网络谱隙和跳跃概率对收敛的具体影响。实验证明，MHLJ算法有效地消除了陷阱现象，并显著加快了去中心化学习的收敛速度。

Conclusion: 本文识别并分析了加权随机游走学习中的陷阱问题，提出的MHLJ算法通过引入长距离跳跃成功解决了该问题，在理论和实验上均证明了其有效性，为去中心化学习提供了一种更高效的优化方法。

Abstract: We study decentralized learning over networks where data are distributed across nodes without a central coordinator. Random walk learning is a token-based approach in which a single model is propagated across the network and updated at each visited node using local data, thereby incurring low communication and computational overheads. In weighted random-walk learning, the transition matrix is designed to achieve a desired sampling distribution, thereby speeding up convergence under data heterogeneity. We show that implementing weighted sampling via the Metropolis-Hastings algorithm can lead to a previously unexplored phenomenon we term entrapment. The random walk may become trapped in a small region of the network, resulting in highly correlated updates and severely degraded convergence. To address this issue, we propose Metropolis-Hastings with Levy jumps, which introduces occasional long-range transitions to restore exploration while respecting local information constraints. We establish a convergence rate that explicitly characterizes the roles of data heterogeneity, network spectral gap, and jump probability, and demonstrate through experiments that MHLJ effectively eliminates entrapment and significantly speeds up decentralized learning.

</details>


### [121] [The Linear Centroids Hypothesis: How Deep Network Features Represent Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11962)
*Thomas Walker,Ahmed Imtiaz Humayun,Randall Balestriero,Richard Baraniuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出线性质心假设(LCH)作为线性表示假设(LRH)的改进框架，通过将可解释性分析应用于深度网络的质心而非潜在激活，解决了LRH忽略个体组件、易识别虚假特征及难以跨组件应用等局限性，在DINO视觉Transformer和GPT2-Large上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的线性表示假设(LRH)存在三个主要局限性：忽略了神经元和层等个体组件、容易识别出虚假特征、无法跨子组件（如多层）应用。这些缺陷限制了深度网络特征识别和解释的准确性与适用性，亟需新的理论框架来提升可解释性研究的有效性。

Method: 提出线性质心假设(LCH)框架，假设特征对应于质心的线性方向，其中质心是对深度网络在输入空间局部区域功能行为的向量概括。方法允许将现有的LRH工具（如稀疏自编码器）应用于深度网络的质心而非潜在激活，并支持新的可解释性方法，如直接识别神经网络电路。

Result: 在DINO视觉Transformer上，基于质心应用稀疏自编码器生成了更稀疏的特征字典，在下游任务上表现更优。在GPT2-Large上，LCH能够直接识别出神经网络电路。实验证明该方法比传统LRH框架更有效，提供了更准确的深度网络特征识别能力。

Conclusion: 线性质心假设(LCH)作为新的深度网络可解释性框架，成功克服了线性表示假设的主要局限，通过质心分析实现了更稀疏、更有效的特征提取，为深度网络解释提供了新的理论视角和实用工具。

Abstract: Identifying and understanding the features that a deep network (DN) extracts from its inputs to produce its outputs is a focal point of interpretability research. The Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH) identifies features in terms of the linear directions formed by the inputs in a DN's latent space. However, the LRH is limited as it abstracts away from individual components (e.g., neurons and layers), is susceptible to identifying spurious features, and cannot be applied across sub-components (e.g., multiple layers). In this paper, we introduce the Linear Centroids Hypothesis (LCH) as a new framework for identifying the features of a DN. The LCH posits that features correspond to linear directions of centroids, which are vector summarizations of the functional behavior of a DN in a local region of its input space. Interpretability studies under the LCH can leverage existing LRH tools, such as sparse autoencoders, by applying them to the DN's centroids rather than to its latent activations. We demonstrate that doing so yields sparser feature dictionaries for DINO vision transformers, which also perform better on downstream tasks. The LCH also inspires novel approaches to interpretability; for example, LCH can readily identify circuits in GPT2-Large. For code to study the LCH https://github.com/ThomasWalker1/LinearCentroidsHypothesis .

</details>


### [122] [Classification of Epileptic iEEG using Topological Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11971)
*Sunia Tanweer,Narayan Puthanmadam Subramaniyam,Firas A. Khasawneh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用拓扑数据分析(TDA)特征对55名癫痫患者的iEEG信号进行三分类脑状态识别，降维后的拓扑特征实现了80%的平衡准确率，经典机器学习模型与深度学习模型表现相当，强调了结构保持降维的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癫痫发作检测面临脑电信号高维度、非线性和潜在随机动态特性的挑战。本研究旨在探索拓扑数据分析(TDA)导出的特征是否能改善癫痫患者iEEG记录中发作前期、发作期和发作间期脑状态的分类效果。

Method: 从55名患者的iEEG信号导出持久图，使用Carlsson坐标、持久图像和模板函数等多种TDA表示方法进行向量化。通过大规模消融研究，系统评估了多个iEEG频带、降维技术、特征表示和分类器架构的组合效果，比较了保持完整多通道特征结构与降维方法的表现差异。

Result: 降维后的拓扑表示在三分类任务中实现了高达80%的平衡准确率。经典机器学习模型表现与深度学习模型相当，最高达到79.17%的平衡准确率，表明精心设计的拓扑特征可显著降低模型复杂度需求。保留完整多通道特征结构的流程因高维特征空间导致严重过拟合。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，精心设计的拓扑特征可以大幅降低模型复杂度要求，经典机器学习模型可作为深度学习的有效替代方案。在将拓扑表示应用于多通道神经数据时，结构保持的降维策略至关重要，能够有效避免高维特征空间导致的过拟合问题。

Abstract: Epileptic seizure detection from EEG signals remains challenging due to the high dimensionality and nonlinear, potentially stochastic, dynamics of neural activity. In this work, we investigate whether features derived from topological data analysis (TDA) can improve the classification of brain states in preictal, ictal and interictal iEEG recordings from epilepsy patients using multichannel data. We analyze data from 55 patients, significantly larger than many previous studies that rely on patient-specific models. Persistence diagrams derived from iEEG signals are vectorized using several TDA representations, including Carlsson coordinates, persistence images, and template functions. To understand how topological representations interact with modern machine learning pipelines, we conduct a large-scale ablation study across multiple iEEG frequency bands, dimensionality reduction techniques, feature representations, and classifier architectures. Our experiments show that dimension-reduced topological representations achieve up to 80\% balanced accuracy for three-class classification. Interestingly, classical machine learning models perform comparably to deep learning models, achieving up to 79.17\% balanced accuracy, suggesting that carefully designed topological features can substantially reduce model complexity requirements. In contrast, pipelines preserving the full multichannel feature structure exhibit severe overfitting due to the high-dimensional feature space. These findings highlight the importance of structure-preserving dimensionality reduction when applying topology-based representations to multichannel neural data.

</details>


### [123] [Multi-Head Residual-Gated DeepONet for Coherent Nonlinear Wave Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11972)
*Zhiwei Fan,Yiming Pan,Daniel Coca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多头部残差门控深度算子网络（MH-RG），通过物理描述符的并行条件路径调控波场预测，在非线性波动力学任务上实现了比传统特征融合方法更低的误差和更好的相位相干性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经算子将输入输出映射视为黑盒高维回归问题，未显式利用初始状态中紧凑物理描述符的结构化信息；现有特征融合策略（如直接拼接或FiLM调制）未能充分挖掘物理描述符对波场动力学的指导作用。

Method: 受量子力学中状态演化与物理可观测量互补角色的启发，设计双路径架构：标准DeepONet状态路径学习波场，并行条件路径处理紧凑物理描述符并作为残差调制因子作用于此预测。具体实现包含前置分支残差调制器、分支残差门控、主干残差门控及低秩多头部机制，以捕获多种互补的条件响应模式。

Result: 在高度非线性保守波动力学和耗散陷阱动力学基准测试中，相比特征增强基线方法，MH-RG DeepONet持续实现更低误差，同时更好地保持相位相干性及相关物理动力学量的保真度。

Conclusion: 所提出的并行条件路径范式与多头部残差门控机制有效利用了物理描述符的结构化信息，为物理约束下的波动力学建模提供了更准确、更具物理一致性的神经算子解决方案。

Abstract: Coherent nonlinear wave dynamics are often strongly shaped by a compact set of physically meaningful descriptors of the initial state. Traditional neural operators typically treat the input-output mapping as a largely black-box high-dimensional regression problem, without explicitly exploiting this structured physical context. Common feature-integration strategies usually rely on direct concatenation or FiLM-style affine modulation in hidden latent spaces. Here we introduce a different paradigm, loosely inspired by the complementary roles of state evolution and physically meaningful observables in quantum mechanics: the wave field is learned through a standard DeepONet state pathway, while compact physical descriptors follow a parallel conditioning pathway and act as residual modulation factors on the state prediction. Based on this idea, we develop a Multi-Head Residual-Gated DeepONet (MH-RG), which combines a pre-branch residual modulator, a branch residual gate, and a trunk residual gate with a low-rank multi-head mechanism to capture multiple complementary conditioned response patterns without prohibitive parameter growth. We evaluate the framework on representative benchmarks including highly nonlinear conservative wave dynamics and dissipative trapped dynamics and further perform detailed mechanistic analyses of the learned multi-head gating behavior. Compared with feature-augmented baselines, MH-RG DeepONet achieves consistently lower error while better preserving phase coherence and the fidelity of physically relevant dynamical quantities.

</details>


### [124] [Exploring Concept Subspace for Self-explainable Text-Attributed Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11986)
*Xiaoxue Han,Libo Zhang,Zining Zhu,Yue Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了图概念瓶颈(GCB)这一新的自解释文本属性图学习范式，通过将图映射到由有意义短语构成的概念瓶颈子空间进行预测，在保持与黑盒图神经网络相当准确率的同时实现了内在可解释性，并展现出更好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释图学习方法主要依赖子图作为解释，存在解释形式单一的问题。为了提供一种新的解释形式并解决模型可解释性与准确性的权衡问题，需要一种能够提供更有意义、更简洁且更忠实解释的自解释图学习方法。

Method: 提出图概念瓶颈(GCB)方法，将图映射到概念瓶颈子空间，其中每个概念都是有意义的短语，预测基于这些概念的激活状态。应用信息瓶颈原则来精炼概念空间，聚焦于最相关的概念，从而引导模型做出正确决策。

Result: GCB实现了内在可解释性，准确率与黑盒图神经网络相当；在分布偏移和数据扰动下表现更好，具有更好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: GCB作为一种新的自解释图学习范式，成功地在保持准确性的同时提供了新的解释形式，通过概念引导的预测机制提升了模型的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: We introduce Graph Concept Bottleneck (GCB) as a new paradigm for self-explainable text-attributed graph learning. GCB maps graphs into a subspace, concept bottleneck, where each concept is a meaningful phrase, and predictions are made based on the activation of these concepts. Unlike existing interpretable graph learning methods that primarily rely on subgraphs as explanations, the concept bottleneck provides a new form of interpretation. To refine the concept space, we apply the information bottleneck principle to focus on the most relevant concepts. This not only yields more concise and faithful explanations but also explicitly guides the model to "think" toward the correct decision. We empirically show that GCB achieves intrinsic interpretability with accuracy on par with black-box Graph Neural Networks.
